**Reason:** Both verses corresponding to U183 and U184 are present between U182 and U185. The two-verse absence claim is rejected. The retained script supplied expected e ma kye kye; this review does not adopt that expected string as fully authenticated.

**Disposition:** Retain U183 and U184 in Adzom; label prior Tingkye absence claim withdrawn. Preserve any recovered old claim only as superseded history.
